Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in biotech theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 929 and the invariant I(A,B) = 520 mod 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the biotech space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 929 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 520. QED.
